# Universal History Part I, II

**Author**: Agapius (Mahboub) de Menbidj le Grec  
**Series**: PO Tome 11  
**Columns**: pp. 8–145  

[p. 8] {#po.11.p8}
“وفى ذلك! الزمان ظهرت فميناة القصومة فكانت تقصم ستة3 انواع وتنقز عقول الناس “وفى ذلك الزمان راى جدعون ابن يواش4 ملاك الرب شجعة على خلاص بْى اسرابيل وعند ذلك قام ودبر بنى اسراييل ارعين سنة
* At that time, Phemonoe the sorceress became famous; she predicted the future in hexameter verses and troubled people's minds. * In that time, Gideon, son of Joash, saw the angel of the Lord, who encouraged him to deliver the Israelites. Then he rose and governed the Israelites for 40 years.
وفى ذلك الزمان انحدر فريسس5 الى بلاد الفرس فاجترٌة راس غرغوا الزانية تلك التى بكثرة حسنها وحملبا كانت تصير الذين ينظرون الها ان يظت بهم انهم من حجادة كالذى تحدّث ددميس؟ الحكيم فى كنبه
At the same time, Perseus went to the land of Persia and cut off the head of Gorgon (Gharghou) the courtesan, who, by her beauty and grace, had struck all those who looked at her to the point that they seemed turned into stone, as Didymus the scholar reports in his books.


[p. 10] {#po.11.p10}
وفى سنة احد وثلثين من تدبير جدعون بنى اسراييل ظهرن وعرفن بنات ايليوس! الاتى ولدت. له من كلناة وه سبع نات أسماوهن قرقاك ومرفاة وهرققا وزرقاة وقردا7 ومديا وسوفياة هولاً ك9 قاصومات وقال انه كان يرى الانسان وجبه فى وجه بعضبن من حسن الوانهن وانه متى كات تقوم احدتهن فى ظلمه كان ذلك الموضع يضى وفى ذلك الزمان بنيت مدينة قورنا0! ومدينة ملطية
Gideon had been governing the Israelites for 31 years when the daughters * of Helios, whom Klytia had given him, became famous and known; the names of these seven daughters were Circe (Qirga), Merope (Mirfa), Hirga (?), Zirga (?), * Qirda (?), Lampetia? (Midya), and Pasiphae (Basufiya); they were sorceresses. It is said that a man saw his own face in the face of each of them, because of the beauty of their color; and when one of them rose in the darkness, that place shone. At that time, the cities of Cyrene and Miletus were built.
وفى ذلك الزمان ملك فسونيوس افوليون!' وقال عليه انه كان يزمر بالقيتار! وهو النى عمل لنفسه عودا من حجارة وكانت تجتمع اليه الوحوش والساع لتسمع صوته فلا ينكى1 بعضها بعضا من كثرة حسن صوته وزميره وغنايه وانه زعموا ريماك! كان يغنى فتنام السباع والوحوش
At that time, Fasunius (?) Apollo (Afulyun) reigned. It is said that he played the cithara; it was he who made himself a lute of stone; the wild beasts and the beasts of prey gathered near him to hear his voice, without doing harm * to one another, because of the great beauty of his voice, his playing, and his singing; it is said that sometimes, when he sang, the wild beasts and the beasts of prey fell asleep.
ومكتوب ان جدعون عكر بساك فى ثلثين الفا وخرج بم بغزواةً! اهل مدين
It is written that Gideon, having formed an army of 30,000 men, went out


[p. 11] {#po.11.p11}
ولان الله لم يحب ان الغلبة بكثرة الجيوش تكون! امرة فاخذ معه لثماية نفس فخلّص بنى اسرايل من مدين ومن بعد جدعون دبر بنى اسراييل ايملك ابنه بعد ثلث سنين فحيث قام ملكا قتل سبعين اخا كانوا له من ابيه جدعون فلما ذهب ابيملك لمحارية قرية يقال لها تبص رمته مرءية بكسور رحاء؟ من الحصن فقتلته وكافاا الله وجازاا وعاجل4 له العقونة بسو عمله والشر الذى فعل باخوته ثم دبر بنى اسراييل ثولغ ابن عته اثنين وعشرين سنة وفى ذلك الزمان اختطفت قوراة بنت زون وفى ذلك الزمان سرق مرسوس الراعى انايب" واحترا ان يجازى افالون وصار ينهما باع ورهان فلما غلب مرسوس صلب على خشية كالذى كان ينهما من لياع
with them against the Midianites. But God, not wanting * the victory to be due to the large number of troops, ordered him to take 300 men with him; and Gideon delivered the Israelites from the Midianites. After Gideon, Abimelech, his son, governed the Israelites for three years. Having become king, he killed his seventy brothers born of his father * Gideon. When Abimelech had gone out against the city of Thebez, a woman threw a fragment of a millstone on him from the top of the citadel and killed him. Thus did God recompense and remunerate him by punishing him promptly for his bad conduct and for the evil he had done to his brothers. Then Thola, his cousin, judged the Israelites for 22 years. In that time, Kora (Proserpina), daughter of Zeus, was abducted. At that time, Marsyas the shepherd (Silenus) stole flutes and attempted to surpass Apollo; they both made a contract and a wager. Then Marsyas, having been defeated, was hanged from a tree, as had been agreed between them.
وفى سنة احد" وعشرين من تدبير ثولغ9 بنيت مدينة طرسوس من فرسوس0! الملك وفى ذلك الزمان تزوّج افالون. امراة!! فولد له منها اسقلبيوس *! وغاب ديونوسيوس على البند وولى عليهم ونا مدينة نوساً! على جانب نهر هندس
* In the 21st year of the government of Tholag, * the city of Tarsus was built by King Perseus.


[p. 12] {#po.11.p12}
At this time, Apollo married a woman and begat Asclepius by her.
وفى ذلك الزمان من بعد مولغ ! دبر بنى اسراييل اير الجلعدانى اثنين وعشرين سنة وصار له ثلثين ابنا فكانوا يركبون معه ثلثين مهرأ
Dionysius seized India, was its governor, and built there the city of Nysa on the banks of the Indus River. At that time, after Tholag, Jair of Gilead judged the Israelites for 22 years; he had thirty sons who rode with him on thirty colts.
وفى ذلك الزمان بنيت مدينة قوزقوة على شط البحر وهى قوزقية التى ذكرنا ان اليت الذى؟ هو احد العجايب السبعة بنى فيا
At this time, the city of Cyzicus was built on the seashore. This is Cyzicus, of which we have already said that the temple, one of the seven wonders, had been built there.
ثم غلب على " بنى اسراييل. من بعد بايير بنى عمون من ولد لوط ثمان عشر سنة وفى ذلك الزمان عرف لينوس7 معلم هرقلس الملك الكبير الاول الذى ذكرنا انه هو اقام الثلثة الاصنام العجبة فى البحر تحذيرا للنواتى من الغرق التى هى احدى8 العجايب السعة التى9 ذكرنا فى بدى كتابنا
Then, * after Jair, the Israelites were subjected to the Ammonites, descendants of Lot, for 18 years. At this time, Linus, master of Hercules, distinguished himself; he was the first great king who, as we have said, erected three wonderful statues in the sea * to save sailors from shipwreck; they were one of the seven wonders of which we spoke at the beginning of our book.
وفى السنة الاولى من غلبة بلى عمون إنيت مدينة صور فلما بلغ بنى عمون بنى اسراييل كل حهد ذهب سكان جلعاد الى يفتاح9! من بعد
In the first year of the victory of the Ammonites, the city of Tyre was built. As the Ammonites oppressed the Israelites at every encounter, the


[p. 13] {#po.11.p13}
خمس عشر سنة كانوا قد توه وطردؤه من عندهم فطلبوا اليه وجاووا يه ' وروسون عليهم فدبر بنى اسراييل ست سنين وصيّر على نفسه لله نذرا وعهدا ومثاقا ان يقرب لله اول من بلقلا من بيته قريانا ذيحا ان اعطلا الله الغلية اذا هو رجع من القتال فلما خرج! وغاب اعداة رجع فخرجت اليه ته الوحيدة فذيحبا وقريبا لله قربانا كالنى نذرى
inhabitants of Gilead went to find Jephthah, fifteen years after having driven him away and sent him far from them. They went to him, brought him back, * and placed him at their head; then he governed the Israelites for six years. He bound himself by a vow and an irrevocable oath to offer to God in sacrifice, after his return from battle, the first of his house whom he would meet, if God granted him victory. Having gone out and defeated the enemies, he returned: now, his only daughter came out to meet him; he sacrificed her and offered her to God in
ثم دبر بنى اسرايل ايصن3 الذى هو محسوب من ايت لحم سبع سنين فصار له ثلثين انا وثلثين نا فرج بنيه الثلثين وادخل ثثين كنّة فى يوم واحد وذّج ناته الثلثين واتخذ ثلثين ختنا فى يوم واحد " ثم دبر بنى اسراييل الون الذى من سط زابلون عشر سنين
sacrifice, in accordance with the vow he had made. Then Ibzan (Abisan) or Heshbon, of Bethlehem, judged the Israelites for seven years. He had * thirty sons and thirty daughters; he married off his thirty sons and, in one day, he brought in thirty daughters-in-law; he married off his thirty daughters and, in one day, he took thirty sons-in-law. * Then Elon, of the tribe of Zebulun, judged the Israelites for ten years.
وفى ذلك الزمان عرفت مذيا القاصومة واحرقت بسحرها قارون" وعلوقةة ومن شدة غضها تلت جميع ابنيها
At that time, the sorceress Medea distinguished herself. By her spells, she caused Creon (Qarun) and Glauce (Aluga) to be burned. In a fit of rage, she caused all her sons to perish. Then Labdon or Abdon judged the Israelites for eight years. He had


[p. 14] {#po.11.p14}
ثم دبر بنى اسراييل لبدون! الذى هو عدون ثمان سنين فصار له ارين انا وثلئين بنو نيه وكانوا يركبون معه سبعين مرا
forty sons and thirty grandsons who rode with him on seventy colts.
وفى ذلك الزمان وقع هرقاس8 الملك فى مرض شديد فطرح نفسه فى نار ومات وكاات حياته أثنين وخمسين سنة
At that time, King Hercules fell gravely ill; he threw himself into the fire and died, at the age of 52.
وفى ذلك الامان عشق افالون واحتب ذقنا حتى انه خرج من عقله ونوسون وحيث اخطاة بنو اسرايل وعظمت ذنوهم اسلمهم الله فى ايدى الفاستانيين؟ اعنى اهل فلسطين احد واه ان سنة ثم دبّر بنى اسرايل من بعد الفاستانيين سمسون” المنذور من ابوية لله؛ا ن ان سط ذان8 فديرهم عشرين سنة وفى سنة ثلشة من تدبيرل بدا بمحارة الفلستانيين وفى سنة خمسة من تديرل كانت قصّة ارعوث الموابية اعنى التى صارت من ال مواب" التى تزوّحها باعز فولد له منها عويد حدّ داود النبى وفى قصص"! ارعوث مايتان وست وارعين اية وكتابها حسن اذا تقل الى العريّة من اليونانى !1 ٠
At this time, Apollo was enamored and in love with Daphne * to such a point that he lost his head and went mad. The Israelites having sinned and their faults having become enormous, * God delivered them into the hands of the Philistines, that is to say, the people of Palestine, for 41 years. Then, after the Philistines, Samson, consecrated to God by his parents, judged the Israelites; he was of the tribe of Dan, and he was judge for 20 years. In the 3rd year of his government, he began the war against the Philistines. In the 5th year of the same, the story of Ruth the Moabite took place, that is to say, originating from the tribe of Moab. Boaz married her * and begat Obed by her, grandfather of the prophet David. The story of Ruth contains 246 verses. Its book is so beautiful that it has been translated from Greek into Arabic.


[p. 15] {#po.11.p15}
وفى سنة ثمان من تدبير سمسونا اخدة الاكسندرس فروسوس بن افريموس4 ملك البون القراين وذهب يقربها الى افالون الالاد" فى بلاد اللاذا وكان ابن ثاثة اين سنة لكانه زعم" هوة الذى اشار على ابيه ان يكون له ابا فلما ذهب ودخل الى ملك اسفريطى8 الذى كان اسمه مثلاوس" راى هناك هيلانى واعجه حسنها وحملبا فاشتهاها فلما اعتقل زوجا “” حيث غاب وتنيجى عنه اختطف الجارية وجاها الى تاربوس0 من بلاد افروجا لدن ابيه ولم يقرب قرانه فلما قدم مثلاوس علم بما كان بحث فاستتجد لمعاوته!! عشرين ملكا مع سفنهم وهى الفان ومايتان وخمسون سفينة وساروا فى البحن وحاربوا افريموس وابنه النى اختطف هيلانى الحسنة! فتلبوا عليها وعلى جميع البلاد التى حارتهم عشر سنين الى سنة ثمان عشر من تدبير سمسون وفى ذلك الزمان خرت مدينة ايلون الموصوفة فى كتاب اوميرس واشعار
In the 8th year of the government of Samson, Alexander Paris, son of Priam, king of Ilion, took offerings and went to offer them to the god Apollo, in the land of Hellas, at the age of 33, because he said that it was the god who had predicted to his father that he would have * a son. When he had left and had presented himself before the king of Sparta who was called Menelaus, he saw Helen there. Her beauty and grace charmed him and he desired her ardently. Her husband having lingered there * where he had gone, and being far from him, Paris abducted the young woman and brought her to Troy, in the land of Phrygia, near his father, without having presented his offering [to Apollo]. When Menelaus arrived and learned what had happened, he sent messengers and called to his aid twenty kings with their ships, to the number of 2,250. They went by sea and fought Priam and his son who had abducted the beautiful Helen. They seized Helen and the whole region that had waged war against them for ten years, until the 18th year of the government of Samson. At this same time, * the city of Ilion, described in the book and the poems of Homer, was destroyed.
وفى سنة تيع عشر من تديير سمسون مات زوس ملك اقريطية ودفن فى اقراطى التى
In the 19th year * of the government of Samson, Zeus, king of Crete, died and


[p. 16] {#po.11.p16}
هى السواحل وكانت حياته سبع ماية وثمانين سنة وانما سمى زو من كثرة حياته لانه انما كان يسمى قل ذلك ديوس وفى ذلك الزمان من بعد ان اباد سمسون خاقا كثيرا من الفلستانيين اسلم فى ايدهم وعتروا عينيه من اجل امراة! منهم كان عشقها سمسون فكانت صديقته فلم تزل تحتال عليه حتى اطلعها على ان قوته فى شعر فلما نام فى حجرها جرت شعرن المنذور لله فذهيت قوته وكتفته ودعت اليه الفلستانيين ومن بعد سمسون صار يبنو اسراييل بلا مدبّر ائتنى عشر سنة ويكون كتاب يوشع ابن نون الاول للقضاا الف وتسع ماية وثاثة وخسين اية والكتاب الثانى الفين وثمان ماية وثمائين اي
was buried at Agrathi, that is to say, on the seashore. He had lived 780 years. Because of his longevity, he was called Zeus; but before that, he was called Dios. At that time, after Samson had caused a great number of Philistines to perish, he was delivered to them; they gouged out his eyes because of one of their women whom he had loved passionately. This friend did not cease to use ruses against him until * he had let her know that his strength was in his hair. While he was sleeping in this woman's room, she cut off his hair consecrated to God; his strength disappeared, she bound him, and called the Philistines to him. After Samson, the Israelites were without a judge for 12 years. The book of Joshua, son of Nun, the first book of Judges, contains 1,953 verses; the second book, 2,880 verses.
ومن بعد ذلك قام عالى الكاهن فدبن بنى اسراييل اررسين سنة وكان ايام قام عليهم ابن ثمانة لثلئين سنة وفى سنة ثمان عشر من تدبير عالى الكاهن ولد سمويل_البى المنذور لله من ابونه وفى سنة عشزين من تدير عالى قرب وقدم سمويل نذرا لله من
* After that came the priest Eli (AG) who judged the Israelites for 40 years; he had become their judge at the age of 38. In the 18th year of the government of the priest Eli, * the prophet Samuel was born, consecrated to God by his parents. In the 20th year of the government of Eli, Hannah, mother of Samuel, presented him and vowed him to the Lord, when he was two years old; and he remained with


[p. 17] {#po.11.p17}
حنّة امه اذ هو ابن سنتين فكان مع عالى الكاهن بقية تدبير فى البيكل لا يفارقه فلما توفى عالى قام. من بعد سمويل فدبرهم سمويل النبى عشرين سنة وكان سمويل يوم توفى عالى فقام مكانه ابن اثنين وعشرين سنة وهى السنة التى قام فيا اخذ الفلستانيون! تابوت الربٍ فلما لم يصبرواة حيث صارت الهم للذى حل بهم من الوجع فى ادبارهم ردوها ومثوا معها القراين تكرمة ليا ومن بعد عشرين سنة ديرهم سمويل سال بنو اسرابيل سمويل ان يقيم لهم " ويصير عليهم ملكا واستعفوا من تدير الله اياهم على ابدى القضاة والمدبرين فصير عليبم شاوول ابن قيس4 ملكا فتكون السنين منذ يوم دبرهم بشوع ابن نون ومن بعده المدبرين الى .يوم ملك فيه شاوول ابن قيس خمس ماية سنة وخمس سنين ومن ادم الى تلك السنة ارعة الف ونائماية واحد وتسعين سنة ويان ذلك على ما تقدم من شرا انا قانا ان السنين من ادم الى الطوفان الفان ومايتان وستة وخمسين سنة ومن الطوفان الى ان ولد ارغوا
the priest Eli in the temple, during the rest of his government, without leaving him. At the death of Eli, the prophet Samuel succeeded him and judged the Israelites for 20 years. When he replaced Eli who had just died, Samuel was 22 years old. This was the year when the Philistines seized the Ark of the Lord; but as they could not bear the pain that seized them in the backside, wherever it arrived, they returned it and sent at the same time offerings in its honor. * When Samuel had governed the Israelites for 20 years, they begged him to name and * to appoint a king for them; they asked to be delivered from the government that the Lord exercised over them through the intermediary of the judges and governors. Consequently, Samuel gave them as king Saul, son of Kish. The number of years from the government of Joshua, son of Nun, and from the judges who had followed him, until the day when Saul, son of Kish, began to reign, was 505; from Adam until that year, 4,391 years. This is explained by what we have set forth above. We have said that from Adam until the flood there elapsed * 2,256 years; from the flood until


[p. 18] {#po.11.p18}
ستماية وسبعين سنة ومن ادم الى تلك الثنة التى ولد فيها ارغوا الفان ونسع ماية وستة وعشرين سنة ومن مولد ارغوا الى ان ولد ابراهيم اربع ماية وسنة عشر سنة يمن ادم الى تلك السنة ثاثة الف وثلثة ماية واثنين وارعين سنة ومن مولد ابراهيم الى ان ولد موسي ادبع ماة سنة بباديسة تعشرين سنة ومن أتم الى أن ولد موسي النانثة: الف وميع ماية وسئة اوستين سئة ومن الثئة التى ولد فيا موسى الى السئة الى اخرج فيا بي اسرايل من ارض مصر وفيها صعد موسى الى حل الله بطور سينا احد وثمانين سنة ومن ادم الى تلك السنة ثلثة الف وثمان ماية وسبعة وارسين سنة ثم دبرهم موسى من بعد اخراجه اباهم من ارض مصر فى التيه تسعة وثاثين سنة فتكون السنين من ادم ومبتدا الخلق الى السنة التى توفى فها موسى ثلثة الف وثمان ماية وستة وثمانين سنة فاذا ازدنا عليها مبلغ سنى المدبرين الذين دبروا بنى اسراييل وهى خدس ماية سنةا وخمس سنين اتبت سنى تاريخ العالم من لدن ادم ومبتداٌ الخدتق ارعة الف زثاثماية واحد سين اسنة وسما كتاب3 القضاة فلما ملك شاوول4 بن قيس من سبط نيامين على بنى اسراييل وتسلط عليهم ارسين سنة ثم حسب تاريخ السنين من اول ملكه وزيدت فى سنى تاريخ العالم قلنا انها انتهت ولغت الى اول سنة من ملك شاوول ارسة الف" وثلثماية واحد
the birth of Arphaxad, 670 years; from Adam until the year of the birth of Arphaxad, 2,926 years; from the birth of Arphaxad until that of Abraham, 416 years; from Adam * until that year, 3,342 years; from the birth of Abraham until that of Moses, 424 years; from Adam until the birth of Moses, 3,766 years; from the year of the birth of Moses until the year of the exodus of the Israelites from Egypt * and the ascent of Moses on the mountain of the Lord, Mount Sinai, 81 years; from Adam until that year, 3,847 years. Then, after having brought the Israelites out of Egypt into the desert, Moses governed them for 39 years. From Adam and the origins of the world until the year of the death of Moses, 3,886 years elapsed; if we add to it the total of the years of the judges who governed the Israelites, * that is to say, 505 years, * the years of the history of the world, from Adam and the origins of the universe, will be 4,391 years. (This book) is called the book of Judges. Saul, son of Kish, of the tribe of Benjamin, having reigned over the Israelites and having governed them for 40 years, one then counted the years from the beginning of his reign and added these years to the years of the history of the world which, as we have said, until the beginning of the reign of Saul, are 4,391. After


[p. 19] {#po.11.p19}
وتسعين سنة ويزاد من بعد ذلك سنو ملكهم ملك ملكا الى ان سناهم يختنصر الملك م من بعد ملوكيم وانتها مدتهم وسبى يختصر اياهم بحسب ويزاد على تارييخ سنى العالم مبلغ سنى ملوك الفرس واهل المشرق الى انقضا مد ملوك المشرق ملكا ملكا ونتتى مبلغ الحساب الى اول سنة من ملوك الطلميوسيين الذين كانوا اولهم بطلميوس الاسكندر وهو ذو القرنين ملوك المغرب
that, one adds the years of their kings, one after the other, until the moment when King Nebuchadnezzar (Bokht-Nasar) reduced them to captivity. * Then, after the period of the kings and after the captivity, the work of Nebuchadnezzar, one counts, by adding it to the era of the world, the total of the years of the kings of Persia and the Orient, one after the other, until the end of the era of the latter; and the total of the calculation leads to the first year of the * Ptolemaic kings who were kings of the West and of whom the first was Ptolemy Alexander or Dhu al-Qarnayn.
ومنسوق ذلك ونخضر ونكتب جميع! القصص والاحاديث والاخبار والعجابب التى كانت فى تلك الدهور والازمان ثم من بعد ذلك نسوق السنين من اول سنة ملك فيبا الاسكندر ” ذو القرنين الى سنينا هذه ونومنا هذا ونخر بما كان فيا ونشرح لك3 ذلك وننه ان شا الله تعالى4
We will recount this in our narrative; we will write the whole of the histories, events, news, and wonders that marked those centuries and those eras; after which, we will continue the annals from the beginning of the reign of Alexander * Dhu al-Qarnayn until our time and until this very day; we will recount what happened then and will set it forth to you in a clear narrative, if God wills.

It is written that the prophet Samuel governed the Israelites for 20 years. They did not obey, as we said at the beginning, neither Samuel nor their

من قولنا بدياا لسمويل ولا لطاعة رتهم وجعلوا يستعفونة من سياستهم وسلونه ان يطلب الى ديهم ان يصيرهم اسون4 الامم فى اقامة الملوك علييم هزياة من عبادة الله وسياسته اباهم على يدى سموبل" نبيه وطلب لشهوة لذتهم7 وكان سمويل يسوقهم؟ ' وعظهم وبخوفهم" وبخرهم بسيرة الملوك فهم ” وانهم ياخذون ناتهم فيصيرونم"! نساجات وفراشات وخازات وطاخات ويستعبدوا بناتهم!! فكان يحذرهم رحاة العطف يم! فلم يذعنواةً! ولا ارتدعوا بل خرجوا الى ان4! قرفوا على؟! ولد سمويل بالكذب ويقولوا عليهم الباطل9! وقالوا لسمويلآ! لداك ستعفى18 منك19 فحيث9 راى جرأهم!2 ولجاجتهم فظهر له فجورهم سال ربّه ان ياذن له ان يصير عليهم ملكا فامر ربّه ان يخرج فيسح ليم شاوول ابن قيس من سبط بيامين ويملكه عليهم لينتقم منهم وكان ذلك من بعد خروجم من مصر بخسماية ومس وارعين اسنة وهى جميع
Lord; and they began to ask the prophet to be exempted from the government of the judges, * begging him to obtain for them from the Lord, like the other peoples, the establishment of a king, thus despising the worship of the Lord and the government that He exercised over them through the intermediary of Samuel, to run after their love of pleasures. Samuel advised them, * exhorted them, warned them, announced to them what would be the conduct of the kings toward them, * [kings] who would seize their daughters to make them weavers, tapestry makers, bakers, and cooks, and would reduce them to slavery. Samuel warned them in the hope of touching them; but they did not obey and did not yield to the remonstrances; on the contrary, they came to accuse the sons of Samuel of lying and to charge them falsely. They said to Samuel: "As for you, we want to shake off your yoke." At the sight of their audacity, their importunity, and their manifest impiety, he asked God for permission to give them a king. The Lord commanded him to go out, to anoint for them Saul, son of Kish, * of the tribe of Benjamin, and to make him reign over them to punish them. This took place * 545 years after the exodus of the Israelites from Egypt, and this figure


[p. 21] {#po.11.p21}
مل سلى مدبريهم وقضاتهم ودهور سياسة رم اياهم على ايدى اولايك! المدبرين ملك بعد شاوول داوود؟ النبى ومن بعد سليمان3 ابنه وسن بعد سليمان ابنه ثم ولد ولده من بعده حتى اتهى الى اخر ملوكهم وسبى يختصر اباهم الى بابل
embraces the whole era during which they were administered by their governors, by their judges, and also by their Lord, of whom the former were the intermediaries. After Saul, the prophet David reigned and, after him, his son Solomon. Solomon was succeeded by his son, and, after him, the son of his son, until the last of their kings and until Nebuchadnezzar had taken them captive to
وفى طول سنى ملوكيم كان اليان البى وعبذياة النبى والعازر البى وميخا البى ويهوذا النبى وعوزياة النبى فلما ارتفع الياس الى السما صير مكانه البشع تلميذه وفى زمان عوزيا الملك تنبًا اشعيا النبى على المسيح ويواييل النبى وزخرا ابن برا" وان البهود رجموا” زخريا ابن برخيا فى البيكل لتعييرة اياهم بالفواحش التى كانوا يعملون فقتلوا فيما بين البيكل والمذيبح فغضب الله عليهم قلط" سلمنسر"! الملك فسبى منهم عشرةٍ اساط!! وهم الذدين يزعمون! اليهود خلف انبر الذى كسبت فيقول الكتاب ان شاوول4! ابن قيسى"! ملك على بنى اسرابيل ارسين سنة فكان ثقمة
Babylon. During the long period of their kings, lived the prophet Elijah, the prophet Obadiah, the prophet Eleazar, the prophet Micah, the prophet Judas, and the prophet Hosea. After his ascension to heaven, the prophet Elijah was replaced by his disciple Elisha. At the time of King Uzziah, the prophets Isaiah, Joel, * and Zechariah, son of Berechiah, prophesied concerning Christ. The Jews stoned Zechariah, son of Berechiah, in the temple, * because he reproached them for the abominations they had committed: they killed him between the temple and the altar. The Lord, irritated against them, put them under the yoke of King Shalmaneser who took ten of their tribes into captivity. According to the Jews, these are those who [are found] beyond the river of Gozan. The Bible says that Saul, son of Kish, reigned over the Israelites for 40 years. This was the punishment that the Lord inflicted upon them to punish them.


[p. 22] {#po.11.p22}
من تقم الله سلطه الله عليهم! لينتقم منهم وفى سنة ثلثة من ملكه حارب العمالقة فقلبهم
In the 3rd year of his reign, Saul waged war against the Amalekites and defeated them.
وفى سنة عشرة؟ من ملكه ولد داوود ابن ايسى من امه نحش4 كان من سط يهوذا
* In the 10th year of the same reign, David, son of Jesse, was born; his mother was Nahash; he belonged to the tribe of Judah.
وفى ذلك الزمان ملك الملوك اللقدمانيين والقرثناين ونيت مدينة نليوس(]5 وفى سنة ثاثة وعشرين من ملك شاوولة مسح داوود_النبى من سمويل8 النبى وكان سمويل يوميذ ابن -خمس وستين سنة وكان داوود ابن “اثة عشر سنة وف سنة ثمان وعشرين من ملك شاوول قتل داوود جلياث9 الجار اذ هو ابن ثمانية عشر سنة
At this time, the kings of the Lacedaemonians and the Corinthians began to reign and the city of Nileus was built. In the 23rd year of the reign of Saul, the prophet David was anointed by the prophet Samuel, who was then 65 years old; David was 13 * years old. In the 28th year of the reign of Saul, David, aged 18, killed the giant Goliath.
وفى سنة ثلئة وثلثين صار حج للانيا فى نابوث" مدينة الرامة فتّا!! شاوول الملك معهم وفى سنة خمس وثلثين من ملك شاوول توفى سامويل النبى
In the 33rd year, a pilgrimage of the prophets took place at Naioth, a city of Ramah; and King Saul prophesied with them. In the 35th year of the reign of Saul, the prophet Samuel died.
" وفى سنة ثلثين من مولد داوود وهى السنة السابعة عشر من وقت ان سح يمن بعد خمس سنين من وفاة سمويل مات شاوول فى قتال الفلستانيين الذين هم اهل
* In the 30th year of the birth of David, 17 years after his anointing and 5 years after the death of Samuel, Saul died in a battle against the Philistines, who are the inhabitants of Palestine. The tribe of Judah assembled and took David.

for his king at Hebron. Then he was anointed again and reigned over all the tribes of the Israelites. || reigned in Jerusalem for 33 years, in all for 40 years. The entire book of Samuel contains 3,436 verses * and the book * of David, that is to say the Psalms, 4,830 verses.
وفى سنة عشرةٍ من ملك داوود جمع جميع بنى اسراييل ودفع التابوت من بيت عميناذاب الى المسكن الذى نصب3ٌ وعمل ليا وكان يتنتى فى ذلك الزمان حاذ النبى وناتان4 النبى واصف النبى وناتان هو الذى جا الى داوود وقال له انه لا بنى بيتا لله لكن يينيه سليمان ابنه" وهو الذى وبخه من اجل امراة اوريا وكان ناتان النبى فى مدينة عون ومات ودفن هناك وكان اصف من اوليك الذين اقام داوود ان يمجدوا قدام تابوت الربّ وحاذ هو الذى قال الله له ان يقول لداوود أن يرتفع فينى مذبحا لله فى اندر اران ويقال ان داوود جمع انبونة والملك والكبنوت ولسنا نحتاج ان ناتى3 بشهادة على
David, in the 10th year of his reign, gathered all the Israelites and brought up the Ark of the Lord from the house of Abinadab into the Tabernacle which he had set up and built for it. At that time, the prophets Gad, Nathan, and Asaph prophesied. Nathan is the one who appeared before David to tell him that he would not build the house of the Lord, but that it would be his son Solomon who would build it; it is also he who rebuked David because of the wife of Uriah. Being in the city of Gibeon, this prophet died there and was buried there. Asaph was among those whom David established to praise God before the Ark of the Lord. It was to Gad that God commanded to tell David to rise and set up an altar to the Lord on the threshing floor of Ornan. It is said that David * united to the dignity of prophet and king * that of


[p. 24] {#po.11.p24}
انه كان ملكا ونيا ولان هذا ظاهر مكشوف وبعرفه كل احد فيقى ان تفحص وندلٌ انه كان كاهنا ايضا فنقول ان المسيح شهد عليه فى الانجيل وقال انه اكل خبز القريان الذى لا كله الا الكبنة فقط وذلك الذى ليس لاحد سلطان ان ياكل منه غيرهم وكان ايضا يلبس لباس الكبنة فى كل حين
priest. We do not need to bring testimonies to show that he was king and prophet, because it is an evident and manifest fact that everyone knows. It remains for us to note and demonstrate that he was also a priest. We assert that Jesus Christ attested to this in the Gospel by saying: David ate the bread of the Presence which no one ate, except the priests alone, which no one had the right to eat, except them. Furthermore, David wore priestly garments at all times.
وكان من المعروفين فى ذلك الزمان ايثار داس الكبنة وفى تلك السنة اعنى فى سنة عشر+ من ملكه رفع التابوت وصيرها فى مسكن الامان اعنى الحجرة التى نصب لبا واقام قدام تابوت الربٍ فى مسكن الزمان مزمرين ومسجدين؟ وسبحين اعنى اضاف واصحايه ومن ذلك الزمان جرت العادةة ان يقام صلوة” ثلث ساعات وست ساعات وتسع ساعات سوى صلوة الصبح والمنا وقبل اليوم ونصف اليل التى هى السبع صلوات فى اليوم والليلة وقد يمال ايضا ان هذ الصلوات ابتدت من زمان انوش ابن شيث ابن ادم لانه
One of the famous men of this era was the high priest Abiathar. In that year, that is to say in the 10th year of his reign, David had the Ark of the Lord raised and placed in the Tabernacle, that is to say the pavilion he had set up to receive it; he established before the Ark of the Lord, in the Tabernacle, singers who chanted, praised, and glorified God, * we mean Asaph and his companions. From that time dates the custom of praying at three o'clock, at six o'clock, and at nine o'clock, not counting * the prayers of the morning, of the evening, of before daybreak, and of midnight: which makes the total of the seven prayers of the day and of the night. It is also said that the custom of performing these prayers goes back to the time of Enoch, son of Seth, son of Adam, because Enoch, the first, began to name and to


[p. 25] {#po.11.p25}
اول من بدا ان يسمى ويدعوا اسم الربّ بان ادم وشيث كان الاسم عندهما المعروف الله فاما انوش فانه هو الذى سمى اسم الرب وسمى الله فقال اليم ربّى والاهى فهو اول من عرف اسم الوب
invoke the Lord; Adam and Seth already knew the name of God (Allâh). But it was Enoch who gave Him the name of Lord (ar-Rabb) and made mention of the name of God (Allâh). He said: "Allahommâ! my Lord and my God!" He was the first to know the name of the Lord (ar-Rabb).
وف سنة ست عشر من ملك داوود مات ناحاش ملك بنى عمون فعث داوود حمنون ابنه ان يعْرى بنى عمون فلما صار حمنون اليهم شجوا4 الكلام ينهم فافترى >عليهم وانصرف وفى سنة ثمان عشر من ملك داوود خرج حمنون الى محارية ايه داوود ٠ فأكترى ملك حزان وملك نصييين وملك ارام بالف ككر من الفضة والككر بدرة فجابهم مع حيش عظيم فكانوا اثنين واثين الفا لمحارية داوود فخرج الى قتليم يواب5 ابن صروياة وايشى اخوا انا اخت داوود وعظما حيوش داوود فقاتلوا حمنون وحبوشه وهزموهم وابادوهم وافتتحوا ارياث7 مديئة بنى عمون وفى ذلك الزمان اغتال داوود اوريا الحاثاى فقتله واخذ امراته
In the 16th year of David's reign, Nahash, king of the Ammonites, died. Then David sent his son Hamon (Hamnoun) to console the Ammonites. When he had arrived among them, a dispute broke out between them. Hamon was unjust toward them; then he went away. * In the 18th year of David's reign, Hamon * revolted against his father David; he took into his pay the kings of Harran, of Nisibis, and * of Aram for a thousand talents (kikar) of silver; the kikar is a large sum of money; he brought them with a numerous army which counted 32,000 men to make war on David. Joab, son of Zeruiah (Sarouyâ), and his brother Abishai, both sons of David's sister and commanders of David's troops, advanced to meet them; they gave battle to Hamon and his troops, put them to rout, exterminated them, and seized Rabbath, city of the Ammonites. At that time, David had Uriah the Hittite killed and took his wife.


[p. 26] {#po.11.p26}
وفى ذلك الزمان بنيت مدن مغنسيا ودورينى فى بلاد اسيا وفى سنة ثلثين من ملك داوود قاتل الفلستانيين! وفى محارته اباهم قنل اربعة حابرة من حاث3 واستاصل جنس3 الجابرة وفى ذلك الزمان بنيت مدينة افاسوس4
At the same time, the cities of Magnesia and Myrina were built in Asia. In the 30th year of his reign, David made war on the Philistines; in the battle he waged against them, he killed the four giants * of Gath and annihilated the race of giants. At this time, the city of Ephesus was built.
وفى ذلك الزمان كان اوميرس شاعر الروم وفى سنة تسعة وثلثين من ملك داوود قسم ايل بنى لاوى سبط الكبنة فاقام ملهم مسبحين وممجدين يزمرون قدام تابوت الرتٍ مايتى وثمانية وثمانين كاهنا وقسمهم لارعة وعشزين قسمة فى كل قسمة منها اتنى عشر كاهنا لساعة واحدة من الارعة وعشرين ساعات النهار والليل يزمر قوم بعد قوم فى كل ساعة اثنى عشر كاهنا ووضع قبايل الكبنة واحدة
At that time lived the Greek poet Homer. In the 39th year of his reign, David divided the families of the Levites, the tribe of priests; he established 288 priests who were to praise and glorify God, singing * before the Ark of the Lord; he divided them into twenty-four classes; each class, which included twelve priests, was to sing during one of the 24 hours of the day and night, one group of twelve priests succeeding another, without interruption. He instituted the tribes of the priests, one following the other.
طلب الاخرى فلما ملك داوود ارعين سنة تنوفى وكاات حياته سعين سنة
After a reign of forty years, David died, aged 70. During his lifetime, the Jews received * a part of the Mishnah, which is the book
وناول اليبود فى حياة داوود من كتاب المثنيةة النى هو كتاب شما من كتب. عننيا وغيره وحماقات! عجيبة لا يجب ولا يحسن ان يكتب فى هذا الكتاب لكى يسمعها منا وغيرها من حماقاتهم المعتوهون الذين قد تمسكوا با الى اليوم كبار علماهم فضلا عن غيرهم من احب واشتاق الى سماعها ٠ وقام من بعد سليمان انه فملك على بنى اسراييل ارعين سنة كالذى يقول الكتاب
of Chema'sa, according to the books of Anania and others, as well as astonishing follies which it is not good and not fitting to make known by recording them in our book. There are still other follies, to which the foolish hold until this day with their eminent scholars, not to mention others who also love and desire to know these [follies]. David was succeeded by * his son Solomon who reigned over the Israelites for 40 years, as the Bible says.


[p. 27] {#po.11.p27}
وفى السنة الاولى من ملكه طرد ابثار الكاهن واذونيا اخاا وتفاهما وقتل يواب ابن ٠ صرونا وفى سنة اربعة من ملكه بدا ان يبنى هبكلا للرب الاله ' فى حل الامورابين اعنى حل بيت المقدس فتكون ملغ4 السنين من ادم ومبتدا الخلق الى تملك السنة ارعة الف ومايةة وخمسة وسعين سنة وفى سنة احدى عشر من ملكه كمل نيان اليت الذى بنا للربّ فكان ابتداوه فى سنة اربعة من ملكه فى هلال ايار فى موضع الاندر الذى اشترى داوود من اران البوسانى فى حبل الامورانبين فناه فى سبع9 سنين وتم ناد فى
In the first year of his reign, he drove out and exiled the priest Abiathar, as well as his brother Adonijah, and killed Joab, son of Zeruiah (Sarouyâ). In the 4th year of his reign, he began to build a Temple to the Lord God * on the mount of the Amorites (Moriah), which is the mountain of Jerusalem. The total of years from Adam and the creation until that year is 4,175 years. In the 11th year of his reign, Solomon finished the construction of the Temple of the Lord, construction begun in the 4th year of his reign, at the beginning of the month of Ayyar (May), on the threshing floor that David had bought from Ornan (Arân) the Jebusite, on the mount of the Amorites. He therefore built the Temple in seven years, this


[p. 28] {#po.11.p28}

construction having been finished in the 11th year of his reign, the 2nd * of the month of Tishrin || (November). It is said that God had inspired David with the idea of buying this threshing floor and ordering his son Solomon to build the Temple of the Lord there, because, as we have recounted above, it was the place * where Abraham had offered his son Isaac to the Lord as a burnt offering; we have described this place with precise details.

In place of the priest Abiathar whom Solomon had relegated, the prophet Zadok (Zadeqiyä) was established in the capacity of priest. It is said that he was the eighth from Aaron. In place of Joab, son of Zeruiah (Sarouyâ), Benaiah, son of Jehoiada, was established as commander of the army. At this time, Hesiod and Homer, poets of the Greeks, became famous.

which is opposite Jerusalem, an altar to Chemosh, god of the Moabites, and to Milcom, god of the Ammonites: * he was obeying some of his wives who had made him impious and had led him astray. At this time, Jeroboam, son of Nebat, fled to Egypt to King Shishak (Chîchäq) and remained there until the death of Solomon and the accession of his son Rehoboam (Ardjab'am). The book of Proverbs of Solomon contains 1,762 verses; the book of Great Wisdom 1,550 verses. The book of Ecclesiastes, which means the sermon of Solomon addressed by him to the Israelites, when he reigned over them and gathered them to deliver this discourse, contains 427 verses. In the book of the Song of Songs there are 256 verses.

Rehoboam, son of Solomon, was born * of an Ammonite woman, Naamah, one year before the accession of Solomon. * Rehoboam, son of Solomon, reigned 17 years; he lived 58 years. In the first year of his reign, not having followed the advice of the elders who had advised him to act with uprightness and to be gentle toward the Israelites,

In the 5th year of Rehoboam's reign, Shishak (Chichäq), king of Egypt, went up to Jerusalem and took all the golden shields that Solomon had made in the Temple; Rehoboam replaced them with others of bronze. After the death of Rehoboam, his son Abijah succeeded him for 3 years. In the first year of his reign, he gathered his troops to the number of four hundred thousand to make war on Jeroboam and the tribes of Israel. Jeroboam, for his part, gathered his troops which counted eight hundred thousand horsemen, and they made war on each other. Jeroboam was defeated and fled. Five hundred thousand warriors

After his death, his son Asa reigned for 41 years; he lived 60 years. In the 3rd year of his reign, Nadab began to reign, after Jeroboam, over the Israelites, and reigned for two years. In the 5th year of Asa's reign, the high priest Abimelech flourished.
ثم ملك على بنى اسرايل الاسباط العشرة بعشا اربعة وعشرين سنة وفى سنة عشر من ملك اسا جمع الاصنام والاوثان فاحرتها واباد الزناةً من جميع مملكته ونحى انه من ملكه7 من اجل انها كانت تعمل الاعياد لاوثان الضلالة؟ وفى سنة تسعة وعشرين من ملك أسا ملك على .نئ اسراييل الا ابن" نبعشا سنتين ومع بعدها :زمرى!1 تعبد. الا الملك فملك سبعة ايام ثم1 اتقسمت مملكة بنى اسراييل الاسباط العشرة فصارت بعضهم تحت لوى تبنى؟! وعضيم! تحت لوى عمرى؟! وكثرت حيو عمرى فال ذلك تبنى فحزن
Then Baasha (Ba'chä) reigned over the ten tribes of the Israelites for 24 years. Asa, in the 10th year of his reign, gathered the idols and had them burned; he drove the prostitutes out of his entire kingdom and removed his mother from it, because she celebrated the festivals of the idols of the grove. In the 29th year of Asa's reign, Elah, son of Baasha, reigned over the Israelites * and occupied the throne for two years; and after him, Zimri, his servant, for seven * days. Then the kingdom of the ten tribes of the Israelites was divided: one part took up the banners of Tibni, another under the banners of Omri.

The troops of Omri being numerous, Tibni was so frightened and saddened by it that he fell ill and died. Omri reigned [alone] over the [ten] tribes for six years. At that time, Omri bought the mountain of Samaria (Chamarin) which belonged to Shemer (Chamir); he built a city there which he called Samaria (Chamarin), like this mountain. It became the seat of the kings of the ten tribes of Israel. The seat of the kings of the tribe of Judah was in Jerusalem. Omri reigned for another six years in the royal city he had built.

* In the 30th year of the reign of Asa, king of the tribe of Judah, Zerah, king of India, moved against Jerusalem, by land and by sea, at the head of an army of eleven hundred thousand men; he camped under its walls * and besieged it. Then King Asa addressed his prayers to his Lord... he took off his royal garments, put on sackcloth, and, humbly prostrate on the ashes, prayed to God, asking for His help and assistance against Zerah, king of India, and against his troops. God, who is powerful and great, heard his prayer, called him, and ordered him to gather his troops and to go out at their head against Zerah, in order to show him His power; but He forbade him

to take into his army people who drew the sword or who launched arrows. When he went out with his army and it came to blows with the troops of Zerah, the latter drew their bows, whose strings made a sound like * the buzzing of beehives. They launched their arrows; but when these arrows arrived at their destination, God turned them back into the hand * of each of the horsemen of Zerah. The horsemen wounded by the arrows complained and informed their king of what was happening to them. "It is one of the sorceries that the Israelites brought back from Egypt," said the king to his companions. The soldiers then drew their swords and began to kill one another, because God had given them, in the eyes of one another, the appearance of the Israelites: thus they exterminated themselves. Those who escaped death drowned in the sea; and three days later the sea cast them back onto the shore. Then God ordered King Asa to go out with his troops toward the sea to seize their spoils. Let no one be astonished at my account in which I record the order given by God to King Asa: every intelligent man knows, in fact, that victory and triumph depend on God. The history of the campaign of Zerah * against Jerusalem and the motive which

Judah for 25 years. In the 2nd year of his reign, Ahab succeeded his father Omri and reigned in Samaria over the ten tribes of Israel. || then married a woman, by the name of Jezebel, daughter of Ethbaal, king of Sidon. He rebuilt the cursed city of Jericho which Joshua, son of Nun, had cursed when he had seized it at the time of the departure of the Israelites from Egypt; Joshua had also cursed the one who would rebuild it. Jericho was rebuilt 700 years after its destruction. One of the prophets of this era was Elijah, of the family of Aaron. He * lived in a city of Gilead. On the day of his birth, his father saw him in a dream, surrounded by flames and ascending to heaven. Another prophet was Obadiah, of the tribe of Ephraim, who had hidden a hundred prophets for fear that they would be killed by King Ahab. * There was also the prophet Micaiah who had announced to King Ahab the victory he would win over his enemies; the Chaldean prophets, among whom was Zedekiah, son of a Canaanite woman: Eleazar with 400 prophets who prophesied in the city of Queen Jezebel against her whims and her passions. In the 8th year of the reign of Jehoshaphat, king of the tribe of Judah, Ahab had built

At this time, the prophet Elijah was taken up to heaven.

belly, which was thus completely emptied, and he died. Then Ahaziah, son of Joram, reigned over the tribe of Judah for one year; he lived 23 years. That year, Jezebel died; she reigned for 36 years, 15 of which were after the death of her husband. It is written that after the death of Ahaziah, son of Athaliah, sister (daughter) of Ahab, Athaliah seized all the royal princes of the tribe of Judah and put them to death, except for Joash, son of Ahaziah, whom Jehosheba (Yahouchaba), his aunt, had hidden with his nurse. * The line of conduct of Athaliah and her goal in all this was to annihilate the kingdom of the tribe of Judah * and to form only one, by uniting her own and that of her brother Ahab, king of the ten tribes * of Israel; then to snatch the scepter from the tribe of Judah, from which it was written that the Messiah was to come, as if she wanted of her own free will to prevent the advent of the Messiah, by obeying the suggestions that Satan, revolted against God, had cast into her heart; for Satan would have liked to render vain the revelations made by God to the prophets concerning the coming of the Messiah, and that is why he made Athaliah his instrument and the place of his residence.

In the 7th year of the reign of Athaliah, Jehoiada the high priest gathered all the

يواش ابن اخازا وهو ابن سبع سنين وملك على سبط يهوذا اربعين سنة وكانت حياته ١ سعة واربعين سن
tribe of Judah, and they proclaimed Joash, son of Ahaziah, who was seven years old, king; he reigned over the tribe of Judah for 40 years and lived * 47 years.
وفى ذلك الزيان عرف لقرعش الذى كان واضع النواميس وفى ذلك الزمان البشع وزحريا! ابن يوبداع الكاهن فامر يواش الملك على سبط بوذا يقتل زحريا النبى ابن يوبداع الكاهن فقتل فى صحن دار الربّ وقتل ايضا جميع بثى يوبداع فغضب الله عليه
At this time, Lycurgus the legislator flourished. At the same * time, Elisha and Zechariah prophesied; the latter was the son of the priest Jehoiada. Joash, king of Judah, ordered the prophet Zechariah, son of the priest Jehoiada, to be put to death, and the prophet was killed in the courtyard of the temple. Joash also had all the children of Jehoiada killed and brought upon himself the wrath of God.
وفي سنة خمس وعشرين من ملك بواش ابن احازيا توف يوبداع الكاهن وفى تلك السنة ملك على اساط بنى اسرايل العشرة بوهاحازة ابن هوك سبع عشر سنة وفى سنة ثلثين من ملك يواش ملك سبط بوذا توفى البشع النبى وكات حيانهة من بعد ان صعد الياس الى السما خمسين سنة لتمام اسبعة وستين اسنة من ايوم مسح
In the 25th year of the reign of Joash, son of Ahaziah, the high priest Jehoiada died. That same year, Jehoahaz, son of Jehu, began to reign over the ten tribes of Israel and reigned for 17 years. In the 30th year of the reign of Joash, king of Judah, the prophet Elisha died, who had lived 50 years after the taking up of Elijah to heaven and 67 years after the anointing.
وقتل يواش ملك سط يهوذا من عبيده ومات ميثة سو وملك بعد اموصيا "انه تسعة وعشرين سنة وكانت حياته اربعة وخمسين؟ سنة
Joash, king of Judah, was killed by his servants and died a miserable death. * Amaziah his son reigned after him * for 29 years; he lived 54 years.
وفى السنة الثانية من ملكه ملك على اساط بنى اسراييل العشرة ملك يقال له يواش
In the 2nd year of his reign, a king, whose name was also Joash, began


[p. 38] {#po.11.p38}
ايضا وكان من اوليك الاسباط العشرة وليس هو يواش ملك سط بوذا فملك عليهم ستة عشر سئة وف ببثة ثلثة عق من. بملكه اموضيا ملك مبظ بيودًا غزاه يواش ملك اسباط إلى اسرابيل واقام المجانيق! على مدينة بيت المقدس فهدم من حايطيها اديع ماية ذراع ودخلبا واخذ جميع ثاب بيت الربٌ وانية القدس وخزاين الملك ورجع الى شمرين مدينة ملكه
to reign over the ten tribes of Israel. But he drew his origin from these ten tribes and was not the Joash who had reigned over the tribe of Judah. He reigned over the ten tribes for 16 years. In the 13th year of the reign of Amaziah, king of Judah, Joash, king of the tribes of Israel, made war on him. He set up ballistae against Jerusalem, destroyed the surrounding wall for a length of four hundred cubits, and entered the city; he then took all the garments of the temple of the Lord, the sacred vessels, the royal treasures, and returned * to Samaria, the capital of his kingdom.
وفى ذلك الزمان تتا هوسع النبى من سبط اساحرةٌ وعموص البى4 الذىة من تقوع وهو الذى قتله اموصياة وفى ذلك الزمان تتتى يونان النبى فهو يونس ابن متى النبى فتنّاة على مدينة ننوى واهلبا وقال ان يونى هو ابن الارملة الذى احياد الياس النبى بعد ان مات9
At that time, the prophet Hosea of the tribe of Issachar prophesied, as did the prophet Amos, of Tekoa (Tagou'), who was killed by order of Amaziah. At this time, * the prophet Younan, who is Jonah, son of the prophet Amathi (Mataï), prophesied. He prophesied against the city of Nineveh and against its inhabitants. It is said that Jonah was the son of the widow, the very one whom the prophet Elijah had resurrected after his death.
وفى سنة ثثمان عشر من ملك اموصيا ملك سبط بهوذا “ملك على اسباط نى اسرايل العشرة يوربعام0! احدى واربعين سنه ولتمام سنة تع وعشرين من ملك اموصيا على
In the 18th year of the reign of Amaziah, king of Judah, Jeroboam began to reign over the ten tribes of Israel and reigned for 41 years. When Amaziah had reigned


[p. 39] {#po.11.p39}
سبط يهوذا! قتله عبيده ومات ميتة سو وملك عليهم عوزياة ابنه بعده اثنين. وخمسين سنة وكانت: سحياته ثمانية وستين سئة فقد دلت3 اسفار الملوك التى4 عنه نكتب ونحدث بقصصهم ان عوزيا هو ابن اموضا واموصيا ابن يواش وواش ابن احازيا واحازيا ابن يورام ولا احد من العلما بشكة فى ذلك فبولاى الثلاث ملوك لم.يكتبوا فى نسبة المسيح فى انجيل متى البشير عليه السلم؟ لان متى قال ان يورام اولد عوزيا وترك هولاى الثلثة ملوك اعنى اموصيا ونوا واحازيا ابو يواش وام يذكرهم فى نسبة السيح
29 years over the tribe of Judah, his servants killed him, and he died a miserable death. His son Uzziah succeeded him and reigned for 52 years; he lived 68 years. The books of Kings, from which we write and recount the history of the kings, indicate that Uzziah was the son of Amaziah; Amaziah, son of Joash; Joash, son of Ahaziah; and Ahaziah, son of Joram. * No one among the scholars has been concerned about this. * But three of these kings are not mentioned in the genealogy of Christ, contained in the Gospel of the evangelist Matthew (peace be upon him!). Matthew says, in fact, that Joram begat Uzziah and he omits these three kings, that is to say Amaziah, Joash, and Ahaziah, father of Joash, and makes no mention of them in the genealogy of Christ.
قصة عجبة من احل اموصيا” ويواش ابنه واحازيا ابن يواش ملوك سبط يهوذا
* Astonishing history of Amaziah, of his son Joash, of Ahaziah, son of Joash, kings of Judah, from whom Christ draws his origin.
النى من سطه كان سيدنا المسيح نصضف وندلَ على الملة التى من اجلبا ترك متى البشيرة ذكرهم حيث نسب اللسيح
We will recount and indicate the motive for which the Evangelist Matthew did not mention these kings when he was drawing up the genealogy of the Messiah


[p. 40] {#po.11.p40}
فى الانجيل وتخظاهم من يورام الى عوزيا لانه هكذا مكتوب اول انجيل متى الذى قال فيه كتاب مولد اسوع المسيح ابن داوود ابن ابراهيم ثم قال ابراهيم اولد! اسحق اسحق اولد بعقوب بعقوب اولد يهوذا واخوته يبوذا اولد فارص وزرخ من ثامر3 فارص اولد حصرون حصرون اولد ارام ارام اولد عميناداب عمناداب اولد نحشون نحشون اولد سلمون سلمون اولد باعن من راحب باعز اولد عويد من روث عوبيد اولد ايسى ايسى5 اولد داوود الملك داوود اولد سليمن من امراة اوريا سليمن اولد ارجبعم ارجعم اولد ابا ابيا اولد اثنا9 اتنا اولد يسافاط7 يسافاط ولد ايورم يورم أولد عوزياة وكذلك نسب المسيح الى ان" اتهى يوسف خطيب مريم فترك متى9! فيما بين يودم الى عوزيا كالذى قلنا ثلثة ملوك الذى ينسب المنيح على هذا القياس
in the Gospel, and [we will say] why he omitted them, from Joram to Uzziah. Thus begins the Gospel where Matthew * says: "History * of the birth of Jesus Christ, son of David, son of Abraham"; then he adds: "Abraham begat Isaac. Isaac begat Jacob. Jacob begat * Judah and his brothers. Judah begat of Tamar Perez and Zerah. Perez begat Hezron (Hosroun). Hezron begat Ram. Ram begat Amminadab. Amminadab begat Nahshon. Nahshon begat Salmon. Salmon begat of Rahab Boaz. Boaz begat Obed of Ruth. Obed begat Jesse. Jesse begat King David. David begat Solomon, of the wife of Uriah. Solomon begat Rehoboam. Rehoboam begat Abijah. Abijah begat Asa. Asa begat Jehoshaphat. Jehoshaphat begat Joram. Joram begat Uzziah." Continuing thus, Matthew draws up the genealogy of Christ until Joseph, betrothed of Mary; but he omits the kings from Joram to Uzziah, as * we have said, that is to say three kings, from whom Christ also draws his origin according to our calculation. Matthew does not insert them into * the genealogy of Christ, and they are: Amaziah, Uzziah, and Joash.
الهم لم يدخلهم متى فى نسبة المسيح!! وهم اموصيا وعوزيا ويواش! فيقول حكما النصارى وعلماوهم! ان يورم فعل فعلا اغضب الله فيما فعله وذلك
Christian scholars and doctors say that Joram did a thing for which


[p. 41] {#po.11.p41}
انه تزوج عثليا اخت اخاب ملك اسباط بنى اسراييل واخاب الملك هو الذى تروج ازال الملكة قاتلة الاننيا فغضب الله على يورام حيث تزوج اخت رجحل قد تزوج ازيال الملكة التى كانت قد1 ظهر من قتلبا الانبيا ما ظهر وانها اقامت هياكل للاصنام وذبايح الاوثان3 فاقدم اخاب الملك منها على ما اقدم
the Lord was irritated: he married Athaliah, sister of Ahab, king of the tribes of Israel, who had married Queen Jezebel, murderess of the prophets. God became angry with Joram because he had married the sister of the husband of Queen Jezebel, as if to have massacred the prophets and erected temples and altars to idols. It is because of her that King Ahab had undertaken what he had done.
وايضا ان الكتاب قال له لما مات اخازيا ملك سط بهوذا خلا يواش ابن اخازيا الذى اخفلا بهوشيع عمه مع مرضعته فغضب الله على يورام كالذى قلنا حيث تزوج عثليا اخت اخاب مرءة4 ارادت ان تميد ملكه وملك سط بوذا وسلهة الذى اليه نسب المسيح وارادت ان تستاصل تسل سبط يهوذا وملكيم وتصيرة لنفسها ولاخاب اخيها وتبطل ان قدرت نسل9 ييوذا الذى من سبطه كان المسيح وتجمع المملكتين مملكة سبط يهوذا ومملكة اسباط بنى اسراييل العشرة” وقتلت عثليا اخت اخاب زوج ازال جميع ولد يوبداع الكاهن ثم ما كفى يورام ما فعل من تزويجه عثليا اخت اخاب حتى
The Bible also says about him: After the death of Ahaziah, king of Judah, Joash, son of Ahaziah, remained alone through the care of Jehosheba, his aunt, who had hidden him with * his nurse. * That is why God was irritated against Joram, as we have already said, because he had married Athaliah, sister of Ahab, the woman who would have wanted to annihilate the kingdom of Joram and that of the tribe of Judah and its posterity, from whom Christ draws his origin; who would have wanted to extirpate the lineage of the tribe of Judah and its kingdom, to make it hers and that of Ahab her brother, and, if possible, annihilate the lineage of Judah from which the Messiah was to come, to unite the two kingdoms, that of Judah and that of the ten tribes, into one. Athaliah, sister of Ahab, husband of Jezebel, also put to death all the children of the high priest Jehoiada. Then Joram was not content with having married Athaliah, sister of Ahab: God was further irritated against him because he had massacred all his brothers, children of his father Jehoshaphat. God therefore brought down upon him


[p. 42] {#po.11.p42}
اسخط الله عليه ثانية بقتله جميع اخوته من ولد يونافاط ابوه فاحل الله به نقمته ومرض ونزل جوفه من دبلا وقى حوفه فارغا فمات ميت سو
his vengeance: he fell ill; his entrails escaped and his belly was entirely emptied; he thus died a miserable death.
ثم ان يواش انه حيث ملك امر بقتل زحرا انبى فقتل فى صحن دار بيت » الربٍ وقتل ايضا جميع بنى يوبداع الكاهن وسلط الله عليه عبيده فقتلوه ومات ميتة سو فلما ملك اموصيا ابنه بعد قتل عموض النبى فى اخ ايام ملكه على سبط .يهوذا وعند ذلك غضب الله عليه! وسلط عليه عبيده فقتل ومات ميثة سو فلمكان هذه القصص وتظاهرها الى متى البشير من انجيله ذكر هولاً الثلشة الملوك من نسبة المسيح حيث به تفرداة من فلاتهم فاشنع -القصص من اخارهم ما همت به عثليا من ابادة سبط يهوذا بما سول لبا الشيطان الساكن فيا ببطلان مجى المسيح فقد شرحنا ذلك لك ونينه فتفهم ذلك ومسل حفظك الله من احببت من العلما والحكما والمتعمقين فى العلم عن قصّة هولاً الثاثة ملوك الذين5 منهم تناسل المسيح فقد شرحتها وديئتها وهى من اسرار كتاب الانجيل مع غيرها من الاسرارة التى7 لا تحصا
Then * his son Joash, having ascended the throne, had the prophet Zechariah killed in the courtyard of the temple of the Lord. He also put to death the entire family of the high priest Jehoiada. Then God delivered him into the hands of his servants who killed him; and he died a miserable death. * When his son Amaziah had ascended the throne of Judah, he killed, at the end of his reign, the prophet Amos. Then God, irritated against him, delivered him into the hands of his servants who killed him; and he died a miserable death. It is because of these striking facts that the evangelist Matthew excluded the names of these three kings from his Gospel, when he was making the genealogy of Christ, out of horror for their acts. The most hideous of these stories is that of Athaliah who had thought to exterminate the tribe of Judah, because Satan, * who inhabited this woman, had persuaded her to prevent the advent of the Messiah. We have already explained this to you and we are now demonstrating it to you, so that you can understand it. Question (may God keep you!) whom you will among the scholars, the wise, and the people steeped in science, about the history of these three kings who are the ancestors of Christ. I have exposed and explained it to you. It is one of the mysteries of the Gospel which contains countless ones.


[p. 43] {#po.11.p43}
فقانا ان عوزيا ابن اموصيا ملك على سيط بوذا انين وخمسين سنة! ومكتوب ان فى سنة ستة من ملكه انقرضت وانقضت ملوك الاثورانين الذين هم الموصليين4 وصارت المماكة الى المدانيين5 باحية بابل واول ملك ملك عليهم فول ابن سا8 قبلك عليهم خمسة وثلئين سنة7
* We have said that Uzziah, son of Amaziah, reigned over the tribe of Judah for 52 years. * It is written that in the 6th year of his reign the dynasty of the kings of the Assyrians, that is to say of Mosul, ended, and that their kingdom fell into the power of the Medes, in the land of Babylon (Bâbil). The first king of the Assyrians was Pul, son of Sa(?); he reigned over them * for 35 years.
وفى ذلك الزمان كانت توة اهل ثينوى المدينة وفى ذلك الزمان كان تنبا هوشع وعمون ويوس ابن متى" واشعيا من اهل بيت المقدس وهو الذى نشرلا منسا بالمناشير 9! وقتله ودفن فى مقر !! شيلوحا؟! ونتى خمسة ولثين سنة ومنع ا من انبوة ثمنية وعشرين سنة اعنى جميع سنى الملوك الارعة الذين كان فيما بيهم وكات حيانه ماية وثلنة عشر سنة وكان السبب الذى منع اشعيا النبى النموة ثمانية وعشرون سنة ان عوزيا ملك سط يوذا اخترا فدخل ليقرب البخور لله
At that time, the penance of the inhabitants of the city of Nineveh took place. At the same time, Hosea, Amos ('Amoun), Jonah, son of Amathi, and Isaiah, who was from Jerusalem and who was sawn with a saw and put to death by order of Manasseh, then buried in the cemetery of Siloam, were prophesying. After having prophesied for 35 years, he was deprived of the gift of prophecy for 28 years, that is to say during the entire reign of the four kings with whom he was; he lived 113 years. * Here is the cause for which the prophet Isaiah was deprived of the gift of prophecy for 28 years: Uzziah, king of Judah, pushed his boldness, while he was not a priest, * to the point of entering (the temple) to offer incense to God. Held back by fear, the prophet Isaiah did not warn


[p. 44] {#po.11.p44}
اذ ليس هو بكاهن! فخاف منه اشعيا التبى وام بحذرة وم يوه وبيويخة على حراته واقدامه على ذلك فنع إثماة من النبوة واخذت منه ثمانية وعشرين سنة حنى مات عوزيا وامتلا جميع .جسده برص وجرب وابيض جلده مثل الثلج حتى عمه فى طرفة عين فلما مات عوزيا ملك بوثام4 ابنه بعده على سط يهوذا ستة عشر سنة فاوحى الله
him, did not blame him, did not rebuke him for this audacity and this boldness. Then Isaiah was deprived * of the gift of prophecy for 28 years, until the death of Uzziah. His whole body became covered with leprosy and scabies; his skin became white as snow; and all this, in the blink of an eye. After the death of Uzziah, Jotham, his son, succeeded him and reigned over the tribe of Judah for 16 years. God inspired Isaiah again, who prophesied for another 61 years.
الى اشعيا فتنبى ايضاة احد وستين سنة وكذلك هو مكتوب فى نوته قال الكتاب ان فى السنة التى مات فيا عوزيا الملك رايت الرب حالسا فى البيكل على منبر منيف9 متعال والسرافيِين8 مصطفين قدامه لكل واحد منهم ستة اسنحة بجاحين مها تغطى وجيه9 وبجاحين تغطى رجليه0! وجاحين يطير !! هذا الى هذا وهذا الى هذا! وبقولون قدوس قدوس قدوس الرب الصباووث الذنى امتلت السماك! والارض تسبحته ونجده؟! وكتاب اشعيا النبى اثة الف وتسع ماية وستة وعشرين اي
Here is what is written in his prophecies, according to the Bible: "The year of the death of King Uzziah, * I saw the Lord sitting in the Temple on a high and exalted throne. The chosen Seraphim stood before him, each of them having six wings; with two wings they covered their faces, with two others they covered their feet, and with the last two they flew, one toward the other, saying: Holy, Holy, Holy is the Lord of hosts; heaven and earth are full of his glory and his majesty." The book of the prophet Isaiah has * 3,926 verses.


[p. 45] {#po.11.p45}
وفى سنة تسعة وعشرين من ملك عوزيا ملك سبط ييوذا ملك على اساط! بثى اسراييل العشرة ازحريا ستة اشهر وملك بعده شالوم ابن يبشو ثلثين .يوما وملك عليم
In the 29th year of the reign of Uzziah, king of Judah, Zechariah ascended the throne of the ten tribes of Israel and reigned for six months. After him Shallum, son of Jabesh, reigned thirty days; then Menahem, son of Gadi, reigned 10 years.
مخيم ابن حجدى عشر سنين وفى ذلك الزمان ملك الملك الاول على الماقدونيين4 اعنى الروم النى5 تسمى اقرونس
At this time, the first king of the Macedonians, that is to say of the Greeks (Roum), reigned. His name was Caranus.
وقال فرفوريوس7 مفسر كتاب ارسطاطاليس8 الذى كان من شماسة كنيسة مدينة صور لانه كان يفتخر على جميع نظرايه من الشمامسة فوثيوا عليه يوما فى المذبح واوجعونا ضري فخرج وترك النصرانية وعاند المسيح ونافق ونقض الانجيل فزعم فرفوريوس" ان اوميروس9! الفيلسوف واضع كتاب اشعار الروم فى ذلك الزمان !! وصف! حروهم
* Porphyrius, commentator on the book of Aristotle, and deacon of a church in the city of Tyre (Sour), had become proud before all the deacons, his peers. That is why they threw themselves upon him one day in the temple and struck him. Then he revolted, abandoned Christianity, denied Christ, apostatized, and refuted the Gospel. This Porphyrius claims that the philosopher Homer, * the author of the book of the poems of the Greeks at that time, recounted their wars.
وفى ذلك الزمان كان زخريا ابن براشيا! وانونياك! راسا كبنة بنى اسراييل ومكتوب ان الهود رجموا زخيا بن براشيا فى يت البيكل فمات زخراة!
At that time, Zechariah, son of Berechiah, and Ananias (Anouniyä), high priests of the Israelites, flourished. It is written that the Jews stoned Zechariah, son of Berechiah, in the temple; and he died.


[p. 46] {#po.11.p46}
وايضا فان لقرعوس واضع نواميس اليونانيين فى ذلك الزمان كان !
Once again Lycurgus, legislator of the Greeks, flourished at this time.
وفى سنة ثلثة وعشرين من ملك عوزيا ملك سبط يوذا غزا فول؟ ملك بابل مدينة شمرين3 التى هى مدينة ملوك4 اسباط بنى اسرابيل العشرة فغلب عليها وافتتحها فدفع اليه ملكها الف ككر من المال وذلك الف بدرة ورجع فول الى المشرق5
In the 23rd year of the reign of Uzziah, king of Judah, Pul, king of Babylon, made an expedition against Samaria, city of the kingdom of the ten tribes of Israel: * he defeated it and seized it. Then the king of Samaria gave him a thousand talents of silver, which makes a thousand purses, and Pul returned to the East.
وفى ذلك الزمان عرف فيدن7 الذى من ارغوس الذى كان يعمل الكيول والثاقيل وفى سنة ارحين من ملك عوزيا ملك ييوذا ملك على اسباط بنى اسرابيل فخا اين مخيم اربع سنين
At this time, Pheidon of Argos made himself known, who manufactured measures and weights. In the 40th year of the reign of Uzziah, king of Judah, Pekahiah, son of Menahem, reigned over the tribes of Israel for four years.
وملك ببابل ونينوى تغلث فلسر الملك خسة وثائين سنة وفى ذلك الزمان ملك اردشير الاول وانقرضت10 ملوك القرنتانيين !! وملك على اليونانيين الملك الاول فى مدينة اثيناس او كوكرييس ثلشة وعشرين سنة وفى سنة خمسة من ملك عوزيا كان ابتدا وضع ستى الكبيس كل اربع سنين سنة كبيسة4!
King Tiglath-Pileser reigned in Babylon and Nineveh for 35 years. At the same time, Ardysus (Ardashir) the first [king of the Lydians] reigned; the kings of the Corinthians ceased; the first king of the Greeks reigned in the city of Athens or Cecrops, for 23 years. In the 5th year of the reign of Uzziah, they began to count leap years, at the rate of one leap year every four years.


[p. 47] {#po.11.p47}
ومن هناك نشت المحارة بين الابراصنى ” والانطى ! اعنى اصحاب الخضرة واصحاب اللون السماى التى كان اول من انشاها ملوكهم لكيما يقع الحرب بينهم فيقع منهم فى المعركة خمس ماية الف قتيل واكثر واقل كالذى وحدناد فى الكتب وكات حيلة من ملوكيم ليلا3 يكثروا فيشغبوا عليهم وقصتهم عجيبة وفها بعض الطول فمتى نشطت لسماعها سمعتها ووجدنا اساقفة هذا الزمان البرم البالى قد فهم عامتهم ذلك واحتذواة به وسمعت بعضهم يقول ايما اسقف كانت رعيته فى عافية وسلامة فهو بش يكون لا ان يكونوا حزنين فاحسب7 ان اكثرهم هذا القياسن حتى قسموا رعيتهم فسمين
From that time dates the beginning of the war between the Prasini * and the Veneti, that is to say, between the partisans of the green and the partisans of the blue. The color was only a pretext that the kings used to spark war between the parties. || fell in the fray * 500,000 men, more or less, as we find in the books. Their kings employed this ruse to prevent them from multiplying and revolting against them. Their history is astonishing, but a little long; if you are ready to hear it, you can hear it. We have found that most of the bishops of that ancient and remote time had grasped this [principle] and held to it. I heard one of them say: "If the flock of a bishop lives in peace and prosperity, the bishop will be unhappy, [and this] until the day when the flock is afflicted." I believe that most of them held to this principle; that is why they divided their flocks into two parties.
فجميع السنين من ادم ومبتدا الخلق الى اول اولمفيا " كانت فى العالم ارعة الف وسبع ماية وخسة وعشرين سنة ومن الطوفان الى تلك السنة الفان واربع ماية وتسعة وستين سنة ومن مخرج بنى اسراييل من ارض مص “ الى تلك السنة ثمان ماية وثمان وسعون سنة ومن ملك شاوول الملك الاول الذى ملك على بنى اسراييل شاثماية وارسة والثين بسنة9
The total number of years from Adam and the origins of the world until the first Olympiad * is in all 4725 years; from the flood until that year, 2469 years; from the exodus of the Israelites from Egypt * until the same year, 878 years; from the reign of Saul, the first king who reigned over the Israelites, 334 years.

In the 50th year of the reign of Uzziah, king of Judah, Pekah, son of Remaliah (Rumalya), began to reign over the ten tribes of Israel and he occupied the throne for 20 years. * In his time Tiglath-Pileser, king of Babylon, made an incursion: he fought the Israelites and took a great number of them into captivity to Babylon. When Uzziah died, after a reign of 52 years, his son Jotham succeeded him and reigned over the tribe of Judah for 16 years; he lived * 41 years.
وفى ذلك. الرمان كان بيثثبا يوايل7 النبى الذى من سيط رونال9 ومييخا0! النبى الذى من سط افريم
At this time, the prophets Joel, of the tribe of Reuben (Rubal), and Micah, of the tribe of Ephraim, were prophesying.

Then also was built the city of Rhodes, which is an island * of the sea. Ptolemy recounts, in his book of the Canon, that it was located in the middle of the fourth climate. It subsisted for 1405 years. Then the Arabs destroyed it and took its inhabitants into captivity.

After the death of Jotham, his son Ahaz succeeded him and reigned 16 years; he lived 36 years. He rose up against his Lord, served the gods of the Gentiles,


[p. 49] {#po.11.p49}
ملك بلاد سورية وفاقئح بن رومالية ! وقتلوا من سيط بوذا ماية الف وعشرين الفا من متاليهم وسبوا منهم خلقا كثيرا" فبعث اخاز ملك سبط بوذا الى تغلث_فلمر4 ملك بابل البدايا وساله نجدته ومعونته فانجده فحيث وصل الى دمشق حارب راصان ملك سورية من بلاد الثام فغلبه وقتله وسبا خلقا كثيراة من اهل مملكته
and worshipped their idols. Then God raised up against him Rezin, king of Syria, and Pekah, son of Remaliah (Rumalya), who massacred 120,000 combatants of the tribe of Judah and took a great number of them captive. Ahaz, * king of Judah, sent gifts to Tiglath-Pileser, king of Babylon, and asked for his help and assistance. This prince lent him aid and, having arrived in Damascus, he fought Rezin, king * of Syria, in the land of Esh-Sham, defeated him and killed him; then he took a great number of inhabitants into captivity
وفى السنة الثانية من ملك اخاز ملك على اسباط بنى اسراييل العشرة هوشع ابن ايلا تسع سنين ومن بعد تلك السنة بعث فاستنجد ادرملح الكوشانى ملك مصر لمعونتة
of his kingdom. In the 2nd year of the reign of Ahaz, Hoshea, son of Elah, ascended the throne of the ten tribes of Israel and reigned for 9 years. The following year, he called to his aid Adrammelech, the Cushite, king of Egypt. At this time, the sage Thales, the first physicist, made himself known.
وفى ذلك الزمان عرف تليس الحكيم صانع الفسيقا7” وفى سنة ثمان من ملك اخاز ملك سلمنسر اربع عشر سنة9 وفى تلك السنة ملك على الروم الذين هم الافرنجة0! لا البونانيين اول ملك ملك
In the 8th year of the reign of Ahaz, Shalmaneser began his reign which lasted for 14 years. This same year, the first king of the Romans (Rum) who were the Franks, not the Greeks of the East (Yunaniyun), ascended the throne. Remus


[p. 50] {#po.11.p50}
على الروم الافرنجيين روماناوس ورومالاوس! ونيا" مدينة رومية العجيبة وكان اخون يملك معه وسمياها باسمه رومية فملك ثمانيا وللثين سنة فوب على أخيه فقتله وقال ان روماناوس ورومالاوس3 ولدا من لفقينا اعنى السبع النى هو الذيب الانشى وكذلك هما مصوران منقوشان فى مدينة4 رومية الى اليوم فاما الحكما فيزعمون ان اموليوس حجتهما ظنّ انما من زنا ولدا فامر من ساعةة ان يطرحا؟ فى الغابة فطرحا وكان اسم اتهما هلانى وكانت كاهنة تقوم تكهّن قدام ارس الذى هو المريخ فظيّ جدهما ان بعض فرسان الروم ألافرنجيين زنا بها وزعم حكما الروم وشعراوهم ان ادس الها فولد له منها روماناوس ورومالاوس وانه لما طرحا فى الغابة وجدتهما امراة كانت ترعا الغنم فرحمتهما واخذتهما وربتهما من لنها ولغة تلك الاتمة فى تلك البلدة ليقونيوس الذى هو بللسان البونانى ذيب يسمون اوليك الذين يرعون الغنم8 وكذلك تفسير ليقونيوس الذى هو راعى من اللسان الافرنجى تتتاول باللسان اليونانى ذيب منجل
(Rumanaus) and Romulus reigned over the Roman-Franks; * they built the marvelous city of Rome. The two brothers reigned together and they called the city of Rome by the name of Remus. (Romulus) reigned for 38 years; he attacked his brother and killed him. It is said that Remus and Romulus were born of an Aukaiva, that is to say, of a she-wolf. This is what their sculpted statues represent, which are found to this day in the city of Rome. As for the scholars, * they recount that their grandfather Amulius, thinking that they were born of adultery, ordered them to be thrown immediately into the forest; they were thrown there. The name of their mother was Helen (Aelia, Ilia) who was a priestess of Ares, that is to say, of Mars. Their grandfather thought that a horseman of the Roman-Franks had committed adultery with her. The Roman scholars and poets recount that Ares made her pregnant, and that he had Remus and Romulus by her; then * that, when they had been thrown into the forest, a woman who was grazing the herds found them, and seized with pity, took them and fed them with her milk. In the language of the people of that country, those who guarded the herds were called Liguniyus, which means in the Greek language "a wolf": thus "Liguniyus", that is to say "shepherd", belongs to the Frankish language and means in the Greek language "a wolf". * They were called this because they always grazed their herds in the deserts and the


[p. 51] {#po.11.p51}
انهم فى الصحادى والبرادى يرعون ابدا ويسكنون فيها بمنزلة السباع والبهايم وقال بعض الحكما ان زوج هلانى! دخل علبها وبات معها فحبلت من ليلتها هما وفى تلك الليلة مات زوجها وتركها حبلى هما فلما ولدت ظنّ امولين جدهما انهما ولدا من زنا فلذلك امر ان يطرحا فى الغابة بين السباع وليس هذا بمنكر ان تكون هلانى حلت يما من ليلتها لان قد علمنا ان8 امرك حبلت من بوذا حيث نام معها بين مفرق الطرق فولدت منه فرص وزارخ8 وليس ايضا نعجب ان يكون مات زوج هلانى من ليلته
wild places and lived there like wild beasts and cattle. A scholar recounts that the husband of Helen (Ilia), having spent the night with her, then fathered them both and that this same night he died, leaving her pregnant. Then when she had brought them into the world, their grandfather Amulius believed that they were born of adultery, * and that is why he ordered them to be thrown into the forest, among the wild beasts. There is nothing strange that Helen (Ilia) conceived them in one night, since we already know that Tamar conceived by Judah, * after having slept with him at a crossroads, and that she had Perez and Zerah by him. We are not surprised, either, that the husband of Helen (Ilia) died the same night.
فلما ملكا جميعا كالذى قانا عشر سنين خرجا الى عداو ومحاربة واقتتالا فوب روماناوس على رومالاوس اخيه فقتله وملك وحد فلم تزل مدينة رومية السنة كلبا فى زلازل ولم _يزل اهلبا بتحاربون وشتتلون بينهم من بعد قتل رومالاون فتضرع؟
When (Remus and Romulus) had reigned together, as we have said, for ten years, they turned to enmity and hatred and came to blows. Romulus threw himself upon his brother Remus, * killed him and reigned alone. But after this murder, for the whole year, the city of Rome did not cease to be shaken by earthquakes, nor its inhabitants to make war and kill one another. Then Romulus addressed prayers to God to ask Him to


[p. 52] {#po.11.p52}
روماناوس الى الله وطلب اليه1 فى كشف تلك الزلازل فارى له فى النوم ان الزلازل لا تساك عن مدينة ملعك حتى تجلس اخاك معك على كرسبى الملك ولا سكن المدينة من القتال والزلازل ولا اهلبا من النقم الا بذلك فصل روماناوس سنما من ذهب يشبه اخيه4 واجلسه معه على الكرسى وكان يجمع الكلام اذا امن ونها فيقول امرنا ونبينا وفطنا وما اشبه ذلك كان يتكلم عن نفسه وعن اخيه فلزمت لبذه العلة ملوك الروم هذا العادة5 فى الامر والنبى ومن تضعيف الالفاظ كمثل امرنا ونهينا وفعلنا وغير ذلك منذ زمان روماناوس الى اليوم9 فمن ساعة اجلس روماناوس صلم اخيه معه على الكرسى امسكت9 الزلازل من المدينة وكفت الامة عن19 القتال
make the earthquakes cease. God said to him in a dream: "The earthquakes will not cease in your capital unless you place your brother beside you on the royal throne; only on this condition will the city cease to be troubled by war and earthquakes and will its inhabitants be safe from punishments." * Then Romulus had a golden statue made in the image of his brother; he placed it beside him on the throne, and when he commanded or forbade, he spoke in the plural, saying: "We command, we forbid, we do", and so on, always expressing himself in his name and in the name of his brother. This is why the kings of the Romans hold to this custom of speaking in the name of two persons, when they command or forbid, from the time of Romulus until today, for example: "we command, we forbid, we do", * and so on. Scarcely had Romulus placed the statue of his brother at his side on the throne, than the earthquakes ceased in the city
ومن بعد ان با اسوار مدينة رومية السبعة واتقن اسواقها وزينها عمل عيدا عظيما ودعا!! ذلك الشبر مرطس *! وهو هلال نيسان ويتفق ابدا فى شهر اذار الذى 8! يكون فيه استوا الليل ولنهار وكان يسمى! ذلك الشهر قل ذلك فريماوس وتفسيلا شر
and the people laid down their arms. When he had built the seven ramparts of the city of Rome, arranged and adorned its streets with art, Romulus held a great feast. He called Mars this month which is the new moon of Nisan (April) and which always corresponds to the month of Adar (March), where the equinox takes place. This month was called * previously Primus, and the translation


[p. 53] {#po.11.p53}
ارس اعنى المريخ وتفسير مرطس ! من اللسان الافرنجى زهرةٍ ألالوان وذلك بان فى ذالك الشهر تظهر الارض زهرتها وترى كثرة حسن الوانها وهذا العيد الذى عمل فان الروم يعملونه فى كل سنة الى اليوم ويسمون ذلك اليوم مرطس وقلندس وفيه يهدون البدايا لملكيم وببضهم لعض وشكئله شكل النوروزة عيد اهل المشرق ثم عمل من بعد ذلك عيدا عظيما واعاد فيه نعيماة كثيرا للروم وصير ليم فاطيا؟ أعنى ولا عهود ومن ذلك الزمان اجروا الملوك العادة وطرحوا الشغب والقتال والاختلاف ين الرعية لكيما يشغلونهم بالحرب ينهم عن انفسهم واقابهم بعضهم بعضاً وان روماناوس بنا فرقس7 اعلى ملعب عجيب فى مدينة رومية لكيما يطرح الاختلاف والمضاددة والمحارة فيما ين اهل
(of Martius) is month of Ares, that is to say, of Mars; and Mars means in the Frankish (Latin) language the beauty of flowers, because, in that month, the earth shows its beauty and adorns itself with a multitude of pretty flowers. The Romans celebrate this feast, which Romulus instituted, * every year until today and call it the Kalends of March; on that day they offer gifts to their king and distribute them among one another. (This feast) resembles that of the new year (Nauruz) among the inhabitants of the East. After that, he held (another) great feast where he distributed many largesses to the Romans. He established for them the ὕπατοι (consuls?), that is to say, the heads of public affairs. Since that time, kings took the habit of throwing division, war, and disagreement among their subjects to distract them from themselves, through civil war, and let them destroy one another. * Romulus built a circus, that is to say, a marvelous theater in the city of Rome, to ward off the opposition, the discontent, and the resentment that were appearing among the inhabitants of the city of Rome, irritated against him because he had killed his brother.


[p. 54] {#po.11.p54}
مدينة رومية الذى! كانوا وا عليه من اجل اخيه حيث قتله وعبل روماناوس لكرامة الس عيدا ويثل هذه الاسطقسات3 بمركية على اربعة افراس ليكون القتال والمحارة عليها وضع لبذه الاريع الاسطقسات اسما وهى الارش فراسينون لى الخضرة لمكان عشها وسما البحر ينطون4 وهو لون السما لمكان المآ الذى يشبه السما وصيّر لون النارة احمر ولون البوا ايض وصيّرت هذه الارحة الاشيا بمديثة رومية الى اليوم واتقسم اهل المدينة قسمين9 وجرت المحارة والقتال بينهم من ذلك الزمان الى اليوم وكان ذلك من حيل الملوك لكيما تفنى الرعية بعضها بعضا فيكون7 ملوكيم فى راحة منهم وستميل الملك احد
Romulus held * a feast in honor of the sun and assimilated the (four) elements to a chariot harnessed with four horses on which one made war. He gave names to these four elements: (he called) the earth Prasinus, that is to say green, because of its grass; he named the sea Venetus, that is to say color of the sky, because the color of the water resembles that of the sky; he made the color of fire red and that of air white. These four things exist in the city of Rome until today. The inhabitants of the city divided into two parties, and since then the war and the fighting still last. * This is one of the ruses that kings have imagined so that their subjects destroy one another and cease to be dangerous, the king always inclining toward the stronger of the two parties.

had them counted for the second time, and their number was 7,000,000 men.
وقد كان ايضا يعمل هونماوس3 ملك فارس وصيره عيدً للشمس فى خسة وعشرين يوما من اذار وكان يصير هذا القتال والمحاربة فى الب والبحر ويصير هذه الاربعة الاسطقسات اعنى انار والبوا والما والارض مستعدة للفسة
* Oenomaus, king of Pisa, had also instituted and celebrated a feast in honor of the sun on the 25th of the month of Adar (March); he had organized combat and wrestling on land and at sea and subordinated to the sun these four elements, that is to say, fire, air, water, and earth.
وفى سنة تسعة من ملك هوشع ملك اسباط بنى اسراييل العشرة وهى سنة ثمان من ملك اخاز ملك سط بوذا وهى السنة الاولى من ملك سلمنعس7 ملك بابل غزا8 سلمنعس9 مدينة شمرين التى هى مدينة ملوك اسباط بنى اسراييل العشرة9! فنزل عليها وحاصرها ثلث سنين وافنتحها وسبا من بنى اسراييل الى بايل عشرة اسباط وبعث سلمنعس !! ملك بابل خلقا كثيرا من ناحية بلاد الموصل وغيرها ليحفظوا تلك البلاد التى كان فيا بنى اسرابيل وسمواة! سامريين الذى تفسيره بالعبرانية حفظة ونواطير فكانت تخرج علهم الاسد والسباع فتقتليم فرفع ذلك الى سلمنعس قال لعل ذلك لان القوم الذى ارساتهم؟!
In the 9th year of the reign of Hoshea, king of the ten tribes of Israel, in the 8th year of the reign of Ahaz, king of Judah, in the first year of the reign of Shalmaneser, king of Babylon, Shalmaneser attacked the city * of Samaria, capital of the kings of the ten tribes of Israel; he camped before it and, after having besieged it for three years, took it; after which he took the inhabitants of the ten tribes of Israel into captivity to Babylon. Then Shalmaneser, king of Babylon, sent a great number of inhabitants from the land of Mosul and other cities, to guard the land where the Israelites had lived. These people were called Samaritans, which means in Hebrew guardians or guards. But the lions and wild beasts went to attack them and killed them. The fact was reported to Shalmaneser who said: "Perhaps this has happened because the people I have

sent do not know the worship of the god of this land?" And he had a certain number of Israelite priests sent to them, who would teach them only the Torah: the wild beasts moved away. * It is for this reason that the Samaritans accept among the prophets only * the single Book of the Torah. They call themselves the true Israelites, but they are mistaken, for they are only the descendants of the people who came from the East.
